SELECT [d].[Id], [d].[Title], [d].[Description], [d].[TargetAmount], [d].[CreatedAt], [d].[ImagePath], COALESCE((
    SELECT COALESCE(SUM([d0].[Amount]), 0.0)
    FROM [Donations] AS [d0]
    WHERE [d0].[DonationCaseId] = [d].[Id]), 0.0) AS [CollectedAmount], (
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM (
        SELECT DISTINCT [d1].[UserId]
        FROM [Donations] AS [d1]
        WHERE [d1].[DonationCaseId] = [d].[Id]
    ) AS [t]) AS [DonorsCount]
FROM [DonationCases] AS [d]
WHERE [d].[Status] = 1
ORDER BY [d].[CreatedAt] DESC

SELECT [d].[Id], [d].[Title], [d].[Description], [d].[TargetAmount], [d].[CreatedAt], [d].[ImagePath], [c].[Id], [c].[Name], [c].[Slug], [t0].[Id], [t0].[Name], [t0].[Slug], [t0].[DonationCaseId], [t0].[TagId], COALESCE((
    SELECT COALESCE(SUM([d1].[Amount]), 0.0)
    FROM [Donations] AS [d1]
    WHERE [d1].[DonationCaseId] = [d].[Id]), 0.0), (
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM (
        SELECT DISTINCT [d2].[UserId]
        FROM [Donations] AS [d2]
        WHERE [d2].[DonationCaseId] = [d].[Id]
    ) AS [t1])
FROM [DonationCases] AS [d]
INNER JOIN [Categories] AS [c] ON [d].[CategoryId] = [c].[Id]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [t].[Id], [t].[Name], [t].[Slug], [d0].[DonationCaseId], [d0].[TagId]
    FROM [DonationCaseTags] AS [d0]
    INNER JOIN [Tags] AS [t] ON [d0].[TagId] = [t].[Id]
) AS [t0] ON [d].[Id] = [t0].[DonationCaseId]
WHERE [d].[Status] = 1
ORDER BY [d].[CreatedAt] DESC, [d].[Id], [c].[Id], [t0].[DonationCaseId], [t0].[TagId]

    FROM [Donations] AS [d1]
    WHERE [d1].[DonationCaseId] = [d].[Id]), 0.0), (
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM (
        SELECT DISTINCT [d2].[UserId]
        FROM [Donations] AS [d2]
        WHERE [d2].[DonationCaseId] = [d].[Id]
    ) AS [t1])
FROM [DonationCases] AS [d]

LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [t].[Id], [t].[Name], [t].[Slug], [d0].[DonationCaseId], [d0].[TagId]
    FROM [DonationCaseTags] AS [d0]

SELECT [d].[Id], [d].[Title], [d].[Description], [d].[TargetAmount], [d].[CreatedAt], [d].[ImagePath], [c].[Id], [c].[Name], [c].[Slug], [t0].[Id], [t0].[Name], [t0].[Slug], [t0].[DonationCaseId], [t0].[TagId], COALESCE((
    SELECT COALESCE(SUM([d1].[Amount]), 0.0)
    FROM [Donations] AS [d1]
    WHERE [d1].[DonationCaseId] = [d].[Id]), 0.0), (
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM (
        SELECT DISTINCT [d2].[UserId]
        FROM [Donations] AS [d2]
        WHERE [d2].[DonationCaseId] = [d].[Id]
    ) AS [t1])
FROM [DonationCases] AS [d]
INNER JOIN [Categories] AS [c] ON [d].[CategoryId] = [c].[Id]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [t].[Id], [t].[Name], [t].[Slug], [d0].[DonationCaseId], [d0].[TagId]
    FROM [DonationCaseTags] AS [d0]
    INNER JOIN [Tags] AS [t] ON [d0].[TagId] = [t].[Id]
) AS [t0] ON [d].[Id] = [t0].[DonationCaseId]
WHERE [d].[Status] = 1
ORDER BY [d].[CreatedAt] DESC, [d].[Id], [c].[Id], [t0].[DonationCaseId], [t0].[TagId]

    ) AS [t3])
FROM (
    SELECT [d].[Id], [d].[CategoryId], [d].[CreatedAt], [d].[Description], [d].[ImagePath], [d].[TargetAmount], [d].[Title], CASE

    END AS [c]
    FROM [DonationCases] AS [d]

        WHEN EXISTS (

            FROM [DonationCaseTags] AS [d0]

) AS [t1] ON [t0].[Id] = [t1].[DonationCaseId]
